📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期麻疹病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 来自接种疫苗的个体的 麻疹 传播 虽然相对不常见,但可能有助于 暴发。荟萃分析发现,消灭地区接种疫苗的传播者比非消灭地区的传播者年龄更大,91%的研究提供了后续传播世代的数据,每个接种疫苗的传播者中位数为4例。
  • 与较轻的病例相比,重症 COVID-19 和严重胸部 CT 受累的患者中观察到较高的抗 麻疹 IgG 滴度。调整后,抗 麻疹 IgG 滴度仍与严重胸部 CT 受累独立相关,但与 死亡率 或 ICU 入住不独立相关。
  • 麻疹病毒 H 蛋白中未检测到 SNP,但 F 蛋白中 30.0-40.9% 的 基因组 中存在非同义多态性;风疹衣壳C和E2糖蛋白中的SNP发生率高达30%,而腮腺炎HN和F抗原中的SNP发生率低于20%。
  • 结果表明,尽管 疫苗接种 覆盖率和有效率较高,但零星或小规模的 麻疹 暴发 仍然存在。 [10.1142/s1793524526500749]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Measles transmissions from vaccinated individuals, although relatively uncommon, can contribute to outbreaks. [10.1080/14760584.2026.2708188].
  • Higher anti-measles IgG titers were observed in patients with severe COVID-19 and severe thoracic CT involvement compared with milder cases. [10.21203/rs.3.rs-10512804/v1].
  • No SNPs were detected in the measles virus H protein, but a nonsynonymous polymorphism in the F protein was present in 30.0–40.9% of genomes; SNPs in rubella capsid C and E2 glycoprotein occurred up to 30%, and in mumps HN and F antigens occurred in less than 20%.
  • The results indicate that sporadic or small-scale measles outbreaks still occur despite high vaccination coverage and efficacy rates. [10.1142/s1793524526500749].

针对 麻疹病毒 血凝素基因的新型 siRNA 的集成计算设计和分子动力学评估

Integrated Computational Design and Molecular Dynamics Evaluation of Novel siRNAs Targeting the Hemagglutinin Gene of Measles Virus
作者： Mohaimen Ahmed Rijvy, Md. Tanvir Rahman Saikat, Mowmita Tabassum, Md. Mosharof Hossain, Sanjida Jahan Shimu, Gazi Tanjim Royhan
摘要中文翻译摘要:尽管存在有效的 疫苗,但 麻疹病毒 (MV) 仍然是全球主要的健康问题,因为目前尚无批准的 抗病毒药物 疗法。RNA 干扰 (RNAi) 是一种很有前景的 抗病毒药物 策略,但针对保守的 MV 血凝素 (H) 基因的研究有限。在本研究中,使用集成计算流程来识别和评估针对 MV H 基因的新型 siRNA 候选物。首先,使用96个MV毒株进行多重序列比对(MSA)以生成共有序列,系统发育树显示毒株之间的遗传差异有限。然后设计并筛选 11 种 siRNA 候选物的脱靶效应。随后,对它们进行了热力学分析、二级和三级结构预测、杂交能量评估以及与人类Argonaute-2(hAgo2)的分子对接。对接结果显示,S3、S4和S7的对接分数分别为-367.22、- 349.03和- 383.07 kcal/mol,在hAgo2的催化PIWI结构域内具有最有利的结合方向。选定的三个候选者在 100 ns 分子动力学 (MD) 模拟过程中保持稳定的结构行为。这些计算分析表明,S3、S4 和 S7 可能作为针对 MV H 基因的有希望的 siRNA 候选物,这需要进一步的体外基因沉默测定和体内验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管存在有效的 疫苗,但 麻疹病毒 (MV) 仍然是全球主要的健康问题,因为目前尚无批准的 抗病毒药物 疗法,并且针对保守的 MV 血凝素 (H) 基因的 RNA 干扰 (RNAi) 研究有限。
设计与对象
该研究使用集成计算流程来识别和评估针对 MV H 基因的新型 siRNA 候选物,从 96 个 MV 菌株开始进行多序列比对和系统发育分析。
核心方法
设计并筛选了 11 种 siRNA 候选物的脱靶效应,然后进行热力学分析、二级和三级结构预测、杂交能量评估以及针对人 Argonaute-2 (hAgo2) 的分子对接,并通过 100 ns 分子动力学 (MD) 模拟评估稳定性。
主要结果
对接结果显示,siRNA候选物S3、S4和S7的对接分数分别为-367.22、-349.03和-383.07 kcal/mol,在hAgo2的催化PIWI结构域内具有良好的结合方向,并在整个模拟过程中保持稳定的结构行为。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为 S3、S4 和 S7 可能成为针对麻疹病毒 H 基因的有前景的 siRNA 候选药物,代表了一种针对 麻疹 开发 抗病毒药物 的新颖计算方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为未来针对 麻疹 的 抗病毒药物 策略的设计提供信息,从而有可能解决对已批准治疗药物的未满足需求,并支持基于 RNAi 的干预措施的开发。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于计算预测和模拟,没有报告体外或体内验证,表明强度较低,需要进一步的实验确认才能评估临床相关性。

Integrated Computational Design and Molecular Dynamics Evaluation of Novel siRNAs Targeting the Hemagglutinin Gene of Measles Virus

Authors: Mohaimen Ahmed Rijvy, Md. Tanvir Rahman Saikat, Mowmita Tabassum, Md. Mosharof Hossain, Sanjida Jahan Shimu, Gazi Tanjim Royhan
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Despite the availability of an effective vaccine, measles virus (MV) remains a major global health concern because no approved antiviral therapeutics currently exist. RNA interference (RNAi) is a promising antiviral strategy, but studies targeting the conserved MV hemagglutinin (H) gene are limited. In this study, an integrated computational pipeline was used to identify and evaluate novel siRNA candidates targeting the MV H gene. At first, 96 MV strains were used to perform multiple sequence alignment (MSA) to generate a consensus sequence, and the phylogenetic tree demonstrated limited genetic divergence among the strains. 11 siRNA candidates were then designed and screened for off-target effects. Subsequently, they were subjected to thermodynamic analysis, secondary and tertiary structure prediction, hybridization energy evaluation, and molecular docking against human Argonaute-2 (hAgo2). The docking results showed that S3, S4, and S7 displayed docking scores of -367.22, − 349.03, and − 383.07 kcal/mol with the most favorable binding orientation within the catalytic PIWI domain of hAgo2. The three selected candidates maintained stable structural behavior throughout 100 ns molecular dynamics (MD) simulations. These computational analyses indicate that the S3, S4, and S7 may serve as promising siRNA candidates against the MV H gene, which require further in vitro gene silencing assays and in vivo validation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Despite the availability of an effective vaccine, measles virus (MV) remains a major global health concern because no approved antiviral therapeutics currently exist, and RNA interference (RNAi) studies targeting the conserved MV hemagglutinin (H) gene are limited.
Design and population
The study used an integrated computational pipeline to identify and evaluate novel siRNA candidates targeting the MV H gene, starting with 96 MV strains for multiple sequence alignment and phylogenetic analysis.
Core methods
Eleven siRNA candidates were designed and screened for off-target effects, followed by thermodynamic analysis, secondary and tertiary structure prediction, hybridization energy evaluation, and molecular docking against human Argonaute-2 (hAgo2), with stability assessed via 100 ns molecular dynamics (MD) simulations.
Main results
The docking results showed that siRNA candidates S3, S4, and S7 displayed docking scores of -367.22, -349.03, and -383.07 kcal/mol, respectively, with favorable binding orientation within the catalytic PIWI domain of hAgo2, and maintained stable structural behavior throughout the simulations.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as indicating that S3, S4, and S7 may serve as promising siRNA candidates against the MV H gene, representing a novel computational approach to antiviral development for measles.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings could inform the design of future antiviral strategies targeting measles, potentially addressing the unmet need for approved therapeutics and supporting the development of RNAi-based interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to computational predictions and simulations, with no in vitro or in vivo validation reported, indicating low strength and necessitating further experimental confirmation before clinical relevance can be assessed.

托坎廷斯 (Tocantins) 实验室物流和监测的优化 托坎廷斯 (Tocantins) 中 麻疹 诊断 实验室物流和血液样本监测的优化 优化实验室物流和监测托坎廷斯

Otimização da logística laboratorial e monitoramento de amostras de sangue para diagnóstico do sarampo no Tocantins Optimization of laboratory logistics and blood sample monitoring for measles diagnosis in Tocantins Optimización de la logística de laboratorio y monitoreo de muestras de sangre para diagnóstico de sarampión en Tocantins
作者： Kássio Ribeiro Reis, Maria Aparecida Batista da Silva, Polliany Aparecida Lopes de Carvalho, Doriane Braga Nunes Bilac
摘要中文翻译高效的实验室物流是健康质量的决定性因素 监测,特别是在地域广阔且结构有限的环境中,例如巴西托坎廷斯州。本文分析了一项优化托坎廷斯中央公共卫生实验室 麻疹 诊断 的物流网络和血液样本监测的提案。该研究基于 诊断 机构,该机构确定了运输延误、缺乏标准化包装程序、冷链故障导致的样品降解、缺乏可追溯性以及与个性化运输相关的高成本。从方法论上来说,这是一项基于机构经验数据、书目审查和技术命题表述的应用性、定性和描述性研究。结果表明,采用具有区域枢纽、路线重组、运营协议和绩效指标的综合物流模式,可以减少损失,提高样本可靠性,降低运营成本,并加强州和市之间的政府间治理。本文的结论是,物流合理化与持续监测相结合是提高公共支出质量和加强巴西统一卫生系统内流行病学应对的相关工具。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了改善巴西托坎廷斯 麻疹 诊断 实验室物流的需求,该地区的领土范围和结构限制阻碍了 麻疹 的高效健康和及时的 诊断。
设计与对象
该研究是在托坎廷斯中央公共卫生实验室进行的一项应用、定性和描述性分析,利用机构经验数据和书目审查来提出物流优化建议。
核心方法
该方法涉及机构实证数据收集、书目审查和技术提案制定,以设计具有区域枢纽、路线重组、运营协议和绩效指标的综合物流模型。
主要结果
结果表明,采用综合物流模式可以减少样本损失,提高可靠性,降低运营成本,并加强州和市级之间的政府间治理。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为物流合理化与持续监测相结合可以提高公共支出质量并加强巴西统一卫生系统内的流行病学应对的证据。
科研与公卫意义
该研究强调了在大型、资源有限的环境中优化实验室物流对于 麻疹 诊断 的重要性,支持改进 麻疹、成本效率和公共卫生系统的治理。
局限与证据强度
定性和描述性设计限制了因果推断和普遍性,并且该研究依赖于机构数据,没有报告定量验证或长期随访,因此需要进行适度的置信度评估。

Otimização da logística laboratorial e monitoramento de amostras de sangue para diagnóstico do sarampo no Tocantins Optimization of laboratory logistics and blood sample monitoring for measles diagnosis in Tocantins Optimización de la logística de laboratorio y monitoreo de muestras de sangre para diagnóstico de sarampión en Tocantins

Authors: Kássio Ribeiro Reis, Maria Aparecida Batista da Silva, Polliany Aparecida Lopes de Carvalho, Doriane Braga Nunes Bilac
Original abstract (English)
Efficient laboratory logistics is a decisive factor for the quality of health surveillance, especially in territorially extensive settings with structural limitations, such as the state of Tocantins, Brazil. This article analyzes a proposal to optimize the logistics network and monitoring of blood samples intended for measles diagnosis within the Central Public Health Laboratory of Tocantins. The study was based on an institutional diagnosis that identified transport delays, lack of standardized packaging procedures, sample degradation due to failures in the cold chain, lack of traceability, and high costs associated with individualized transportation. Methodologically, this is an applied, qualitative, and descriptive study grounded in institutional empirical data, bibliographic review, and technical-propositional formulation. The results indicate that adopting an integrated logistics model, with regional hubs, route reorganization, operational protocols, and performance indicators, may reduce losses, improve sample reliability, lower operational costs, and strengthen intergovernmental governance between the state and municipalities. The article concludes that logistics rationalization combined with continuous monitoring is a relevant instrument for improving public expenditure quality and strengthening the epidemiological response within Brazil’s Unified Health System.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the need to improve laboratory logistics for measles diagnosis in Tocantins, Brazil, where territorial extent and structural limitations hinder efficient health surveillance and timely diagnosis.
Design and population
The study was an applied, qualitative, and descriptive analysis conducted at the Central Public Health Laboratory of Tocantins, using institutional empirical data and a bibliographic review to propose logistics optimization.
Core methods
The methodology involved institutional empirical data collection, bibliographic review, and technical-propositional formulation to design an integrated logistics model with regional hubs, route reorganization, operational protocols, and performance indicators.
Main results
The results indicated that adopting an integrated logistics model may reduce sample losses, improve reliability, lower operational costs, and strengthen intergovernmental governance between state and municipal levels.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence that logistics rationalization combined with continuous monitoring can enhance public expenditure quality and strengthen the epidemiological response within Brazil’s Unified Health System.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study highlights the importance of optimized laboratory logistics for measles diagnosis in large, resource-limited settings, supporting improved surveillance, cost efficiency, and governance in public health systems.
Limitations and evidence strength
The qualitative and descriptive design limits causal inference and generalizability, and the study relies on institutional data without reported quantitative validation or long-term follow-up, warranting a moderate confidence assessment.

通过高通量测序对 麻疹、风疹和腮腺炎病毒生产株进行遗传表征

Genetic characterization of production strains of measles, rubella, and mumps viruses by high-throughput sequencing
作者： A. I. Zheltukhina, Andrey B. Komissarov, A. V. Fadeev, Nikita Yolshin, M. M. Pisareva, Veronica Eder, Margarita Korzhanova, A. D. Ksenafontov, A. A. Perederiy, Olesia V. Ohlopkova
摘要中文翻译自发的 突变 经常在含 RNA 病毒的 基因组 中产生和积累,例如 麻疹、风疹和腮腺炎。生产含有活 疫苗 病毒的 疫苗 产品的现代要求建议控制生产菌株的遗传稳定性。高通量测序方法可以确定病毒群体中出现的所有 突变,并评估生产菌株的遗传稳定性。对麻疹(Leningrad-16)、风疹(RA27/3)和流行性腮腺炎(Leningrad-3)病毒种子株和生产株基因组的核苷酸序列进行分析,并与疫苗株基因组的参考核苷酸序列(在Gen-Bank数据库中提交)进行比较。材料和方法。本研究使用疫苗生产菌株(麻疹培养活体、风疹培养活体和流行性腮腺炎培养活体)。使用 MGI DNBSEQ G-400 和 Illumina NextSeq2000 遗传分析仪进行整个 基因组 测序。在处理和分析测序数据时,使用了以下程序和工具:FastQC v.0.12.1、FastP v.0.23.4、BWA-MEM2 v.2.2.1、VizCoV 工具。对研究样本中病毒群体内的单核苷酸多态性 (SNP) 的分析揭示了许多微小变异,这些变异在进一步积累后可能导致 疫苗 毒株蛋白的共有序列发生变化。对于所研究的麻疹病毒样本,在主要麻疹病毒抗原H蛋白中未发现SNP,然而,在F蛋白中检测到非同义多态性,该多态性出现在病毒基因组的30.0-40.9%中。SNP 在风疹病毒的衣壳蛋白 C 和表面糖蛋白 E2 中被发现,在病毒群体中出现的频率高达 30%。在腮腺炎病毒的表面抗原血凝素神经氨酸酶 HN 和融合蛋白 F 中发现了 SNP,出现在不到 20% 病毒群体的 基因组 中。生产菌株基因组中未检出影响麻疹、风疹、流行性腮腺炎疫苗免疫原性的突变。已鉴定出的一组微小变异在病毒群体中出现频率超过10%,随着进一步积累,可导致疫苗毒株共有序列发生变化,使得利用高通量测序监测疫苗毒株稳定性具有相关性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
RNA 病毒(例如 麻疹病毒)可以自发积累 突变,这可能会损害 疫苗 菌株的稳定性和功效,从而需要对生产菌株进行基因监测。
设计与对象
该研究利用高通量测序对 麻疹 (Leningrad-16)、风疹 (RA27/3) 和腮腺炎 (Leningrad-3) 病毒的种子和生产菌株进行了表征,以评估遗传稳定性。
核心方法
整个基因组测序在MGI DNBSEQ G-400和Illumina NextSeq2000平台上进行,使用FastQC、FastP、BWA-MEM2和VizCoV工具处理数据以识别单核苷酸多态性。
主要结果
麻疹病毒 H 蛋白中未检测到 SNP,但 F 蛋白中 30.0-40.9% 的 基因组 中存在非同义多态性;风疹衣壳C和E2糖蛋白中的SNP发生率高达30%,而腮腺炎HN和F抗原中的SNP发生率低于20%。
解释与创新
作者将微小变异检测解释为高通量测序可以监测 疫苗 菌株稳定性的证据,强调了其与早期检测潜在免疫原性改变 突变 的相关性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持麻疹、风疹和流行性腮腺炎疫苗生产菌株的常规基因组监测,以确保遗传稳定性并维持疫苗免疫原性。
局限与证据强度
该研究没有评估检测到的多态性对 疫苗 免疫原性的功能影响,限制了因果推断;由于缺乏功能分析,证据强度中等。

Genetic characterization of production strains of measles, rubella, and mumps viruses by high-throughput sequencing

Authors: A. I. Zheltukhina, Andrey B. Komissarov, A. V. Fadeev, Nikita Yolshin, M. M. Pisareva, Veronica Eder, Margarita Korzhanova, A. D. Ksenafontov, A. A. Perederiy, Olesia V. Ohlopkova
Original abstract (English)
Spontaneous mutations often arise and accumulate in the genome of RNA-containing viruses, for example measles, rubella and mumps. Modern requirements for the production of vaccine products containing live vaccine viruses suggest control of the genetic stability of production strains. The method of high throughput sequencing allows to determine all mutations arisen in viral population, and to assess genetic stability of production strains. Analysis of nucleotide sequence of the genomes of seed and production strains of measles (Leningrad-16), rubella (RA27/3) and mumps (Leningrad-3) viruses, in comparison with the reference nucleotide sequences of vaccine strains genomes (submitted in the Gen-Bank database). MATERIALS AND METHODS. Production strains of vaccines (measles culture live, rubella culture live and mumps culture live) were used in this study. Whole genome sequencing was performed using the MGI DNBSEQ G-400 and Illumina NextSeq2000 genetic analyzers. When processing and analyzing the sequencing data, the following programs and tools were used: FastQC v.0.12.1, FastP v.0.23.4, BWA-MEM2 v.2.2.1, VizCoV tool. Analysis of single nucleotide polymorphisms (SNPs) within the viral population in the studied samples revealed a number of minor variants that, and after further accumulation, may lead to changes in the consensus sequences of the vaccine strains proteins. For the studied measles virus samples, no SNPs were found in the main measles virus antigen H protein, however, a nonsynonymous polymorphism was detected in the F protein, occurring in 30.0–40.9% of the viral genomes. SNPs were found in the capsid protein C and in the surface glycoprotein E2 rubella virus, occurring in the viral population with a frequency of up to 30%. SNPs were found in surface antigens hemagglutinin neuraminidase HN and fusion protein F of mumps virus, occurring in the genomes of less than 20% of viral population. Mutations in the genomes of production strains that could affect the immunogenicity of measles, rubella, and mumps vaccines were not detected. The identified set of minor variants with a frequency of occurrence in viral population of more than 10%, which, with further accumulation, can lead to changes in the consensus sequences of vaccine strains, makes the monitoring of vaccine strains stability using high-throughput sequencing relevant.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
RNA viruses such as measles virus can accumulate spontaneous mutations that may compromise vaccine strain stability and efficacy, creating a need for genetic monitoring of production strains.
Design and population
The study characterized seed and production strains of measles (Leningrad-16), rubella (RA27/3), and mumps (Leningrad-3) viruses using high-throughput sequencing to assess genetic stability.
Core methods
Whole genome sequencing was performed on the MGI DNBSEQ G-400 and Illumina NextSeq2000 platforms, with data processed using FastQC, FastP, BWA-MEM2, and VizCoV tools to identify single nucleotide polymorphisms.
Main results
No SNPs were detected in the measles virus H protein, but a nonsynonymous polymorphism in the F protein was present in 30.0–40.9% of genomes; SNPs in rubella capsid C and E2 glycoprotein occurred up to 30%, and in mumps HN and F antigens occurred in less than 20%.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret minor variant detection as evidence that high-throughput sequencing can monitor vaccine strain stability, highlighting its relevance for early detection of potential immunogenicity-altering mutations.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support routine genomic surveillance of measles, rubella, and mumps vaccine production strains to ensure genetic stability and maintain vaccine immunogenicity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study did not evaluate functional effects of detected polymorphisms on vaccine immunogenicity, limiting causal inference; evidence strength is moderate given the absence of functional assays.

麻疹 IgG 抗体滴度与住院 COVID-19 患者胸部 CT 严重程度的关系

Association of Measles IgG Antibody Titers with Thoracic CT Severity in Hospitalized Patients with COVID-19
作者： Bennur Esen, Emel Saglam, Arif Savas, Deniz Oke, Erdal Gundogan, Zeyneb Tuzcu, Seyda Andac, Ahmet Engin Atay, Emel Sağlam, Deniz Öke
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 2019 年冠状病毒病 (COVID-19) 具有广泛的临床谱,从轻微的呼吸道症状到严重的肺炎和死亡。先前的研究表明,在 SARS-CoV-2 感染期间,针对 麻疹 的预先存在的免疫力可能会影响 麻疹;然而,COVID-19 中抗 麻疹 IgG 滴度的临床意义仍不清楚。因此,我们的目的是调查住院 COVID-19 患者中抗 麻疹 IgG 滴度、临床严重程度、COVID-19 死亡率 率 (CMR) 评分、重症监护病房 (ICU) 要求和 死亡率 之间的关联。材料和方法 这项横断面研究包括 153 名住院的 COVID-19 患者(86 名男性和 67 名女性)。疾病严重程度根据 NIH COVID-19 治疗 指南进行分类。使用间接 ELISA 测定法测量抗 麻疹 IgG 水平。使用 COVID-19 死亡率 率 (CMR) 评分评估 死亡率 风险。结果 与病情较轻的患者相比,严重 COVID-19 和严重胸部 CT 受累的患者中观察到较高的抗 麻疹 IgG 滴度。与非严重受累患者相比,严重 CT 受累患者的抗 麻疹 IgG 滴度显着较高,并且在调整临床和实验室参数后仍与严重胸部 CT 受累独立相关。然而,抗 麻疹 IgG 水平与 死亡率 或 ICU 入住并不独立相关。与幸存者相比,非幸存者年龄更大、住院时间更长、CMR 评分更高、入院 SpO2 水平更低。同样,需要入住 ICU 的患者具有较高的 CMR 评分和更严重的胸部 CT 受累。结论 较高的抗 麻疹 IgG 滴度与住院 COVID-19 患者严重胸部 CT 受累独立相关。这些发现表明,抗 麻疹 IgG 水平可能代表与肺部疾病严重程度相关的潜在免疫标志物,而不是 死亡率 风险的直接标志物。需要对更多人群进行进一步的前瞻性研究,以阐明抗 麻疹 IgG 反应在 COVID-19 中的临床意义。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
COVID-19 具有广泛的临床谱,但先前存在的抗 麻疹 免疫对 SARS-CoV-2 疾病严重程度的影响仍不确定。该研究试图澄清抗 麻疹 IgG 滴度是否与住院 COVID-19 患者的临床严重程度、死亡率 风险、ICU 需求或胸部 CT 参与相关。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究招募了 153 名住院的 COVID-19 患者(86 名男性和 67 名女性),并根据 NIH COVID-19 治疗 指南对疾病严重程度进行分类。
核心方法
使用间接 ELISA 测定对抗 麻疹 IgG 水平进行定量,并使用 COVID-19 死亡率 率 (CMR) 评分评估 死亡率 风险。调整临床和实验室参数后评估关联性。
主要结果
与较轻的病例相比,重症 COVID-19 和严重胸部 CT 受累的患者中观察到较高的抗 麻疹 IgG 滴度。调整后,抗 麻疹 IgG 滴度仍与严重胸部 CT 受累独立相关,但与 死亡率 或 ICU 入住不独立相关。
解释与创新
作者将抗 麻疹 IgG 滴度解释为与肺部疾病严重程度相关的潜在免疫标志物,而不是与 ZX​​QTERMQQXZ 风险相关的潜在免疫标志物。这是第一份关于抗 麻疹 IgG 水平与住院 COVID-19 患者严重胸部 CT 受累之间存在独立关联的报告。
科研与公卫意义
如果经过验证,抗 麻疹 IgG 滴度可以为 COVID-19 肺部并发症的风险分层提供信息,从而有可能指导住院患者的监测或治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
横截面设计限制了因果推断,单中心样本可能会降低普遍性。由于这些局限性和缺乏前瞻性验证,证据被评估为中等。

Association of Measles IgG Antibody Titers with Thoracic CT Severity in Hospitalized Patients with COVID-19

Authors: Bennur Esen, Emel Saglam, Arif Savas, Deniz Oke, Erdal Gundogan, Zeyneb Tuzcu, Seyda Andac, Ahmet Engin Atay, Emel Sağlam, Deniz Öke
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) has a broad clinical spectrum ranging from mild respiratory symptoms to severe pneumonia and death. Previous studies have suggested that pre-existing immunity against measles may influence immune responses during SARS-CoV-2 infection; however, the clinical significance of anti-measles IgG titers in COVID-19 remains unclear. Therefore, we aimed to investigate the association between anti-measles IgG titers, clinical severity, COVID-19 Mortality Rate (CMR) score, intensive care unit (ICU) requirement, and mortality in hospitalized COVID-19 patients. Material and Methods This cross-sectional study included 153 hospitalized COVID-19 patients (86 males and 67 females). Disease severity was classified according to the NIH COVID-19 Treatment Guidelines. Anti-measles IgG levels were measured using an indirect ELISA assay. Mortality risk was assessed using the COVID-19 Mortality Rate (CMR) score. Results Higher anti-measles IgG titers were observed in patients with severe COVID-19 and severe thoracic CT involvement compared with patients with milder disease. Anti-measles IgG titers were significantly higher in patients with severe CT involvement compared with those with non-severe involvement and remained independently associated with severe thoracic CT involvement after adjustment for clinical and laboratory parameters. However, anti-measles IgG levels were not independently associated with mortality or ICU admission. Non-survivors were older, had longer hospital stays, higher CMR scores, and lower admission SpO2 levels compared with survivors. Similarly, patients requiring ICU admission had higher CMR scores and more severe thoracic CT involvement. Conclusion Higher anti-measles IgG titers were independently associated with severe thoracic CT involvement in hospitalized COVID-19 patients. These findings suggest that anti-measles IgG levels may represent a potential immunological marker associated with pulmonary disease severity rather than a direct marker of mortality risk. Further prospective studies with larger populations are needed to clarify the clinical significance of anti-measles IgG responses in COVID-19.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
COVID-19 presents with a wide clinical spectrum, yet the influence of pre-existing anti-measles immunity on SARS-CoV-2 disease severity remains uncertain. The study sought to clarify whether anti-measles IgG titers are associated with clinical severity, mortality risk, ICU requirement, or thoracic CT involvement in hospitalized COVID-19 patients.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study enrolled 153 hospitalized COVID-19 patients (86 males and 67 females) and classified disease severity according to NIH COVID-19 Treatment Guidelines.
Core methods
Anti-measles IgG levels were quantified using an indirect ELISA assay, and mortality risk was assessed with the COVID-19 Mortality Rate (CMR) score. Associations were evaluated after adjustment for clinical and laboratory parameters.
Main results
Higher anti-measles IgG titers were observed in patients with severe COVID-19 and severe thoracic CT involvement compared with milder cases. Anti-measles IgG titers remained independently associated with severe thoracic CT involvement after adjustment, but were not independently associated with mortality or ICU admission.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret anti-measles IgG titers as a potential immunological marker linked to pulmonary disease severity rather than mortality risk. This is the first report of an independent association between anti-measles IgG levels and severe thoracic CT involvement in hospitalized COVID-19 patients.
Scientific and public-health significance
If validated, anti-measles IgG titers could inform risk stratification for pulmonary complications in COVID-19, potentially guiding monitoring or therapeutic strategies in hospitalized patients.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design limits causal inference, and the single-center sample may reduce generalizability. The evidence is assessed as moderate due to these limitations and the absence of prospective validation.

2023 年刚果民主共和国南基伍省疑似和实验室确诊 麻疹 病例的临床和流行病学概况

Clinical and epidemiological profile of suspected and laboratory-confirmed measles cases in South Kivu, Democratic Republic of the Congo, 2023
作者： Matundanya Assumani Joseph, Mulume Musafiri Eric, Bashizi Cito, Christian Ciribagula, Jonathan Tshongo, Kiza Mulambe Daddy, Ngaboyeka Gaylord, Masimango Imani Mannix, Pacifique Mwene-Batu, Lupande Mwenebitu David
摘要中文翻译尽管 麻疹 不断做出努力,但 麻疹 仍然是许多资源匮乏地区的重大公共卫生挑战。2023 年,在针对 6-59 个月大儿童开展大规模 麻疹 运动后,南基伍省(刚果民主共和国)报告疑似 麻疹 病例再次出现。本研究旨在描述疑似 麻疹 病例的临床和流行病学特征,并确定活动后与 麻疹 IgM 阳性相关的因素。在受 麻疹 影响最严重的五个卫生区(卡莱赫、米诺瓦、伊吉维、米蒂-穆尔赫萨和卡隆格),对出现 麻疹 临床症状的 6-59 个月儿童进行了一项横断面研究。疑似病例是从当地卫生部门提供的名单中详尽登记的。获得父母同意后,收集血液样本并在国家生物医学研究所 (INRB) 检测 麻疹 和风疹 IgM 抗体。使用 R 版本 4.3.3 分析数据。使用逻辑回归来识别疑似病例中与 麻疹 IgM 阳性相关的特征。在接受测试的 82 名儿童中,39 名 (48%) 的 麻疹 IgM 呈阳性。43 名 IgM 阴性儿童中,8 名(18%)风疹 IgM 阳性。在调整分析中,麻疹 IgM 阳性显示证据表明,麻疹 IgM 阳性与男性父母对儿童 疫苗接种(aOR:7.22;95% CI:1.64–43.2;p = 0.015)和其他工人(aOR:8.28;95% CI:1.19-48;p = < 0.008)。尽管据报道 麻疹 的行政覆盖率很高,但 麻疹 继续在南基伍省的儿童中传播。加强免疫服务质量、扩大对未接种疫苗儿童的覆盖范围以及让男性家长和其他工作人员参与可能会提高 疫苗 的接受度并减少活动后期的 麻疹 传播。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管开展了 麻疹 活动,但 麻疹 仍继续在资源匮乏的环境中构成公共卫生负担,从而给活动后 传播 动态和感染决定因素带来不确定性。该研究解决了刚果民主共和国南基伍省疑似 麻疹 病例的临床和流行病学概况以及与实验室确认相关因素的知识差距。
设计与对象
在南基伍省的五个卫生区(Kalehe、Minova、Idjwi、Miti-Murhesa 和 Kalonge)对出现 麻疹 临床症状的 6-59 个月儿童进行了一项横断面研究。疑似病例是从当地卫生部门提供的名单中详尽登记的。
核心方法
在获得父母同意后收集血样,并在国家生物医学研究所 (INRB) 检测 麻疹 和风疹 IgM 抗体。使用 R 版本 4.3.3 分析数据,并使用逻辑回归来识别疑似病例中与 麻疹 IgM 阳性相关的特征。
主要结果
在接受测试的 82 名儿童中,39 名 (48%) 的 麻疹 IgM 呈阳性。43 名 IgM 阴性儿童中,8 名(18%)风疹 IgM 阳性。在调整分析中,麻疹 IgM 阳性显示与男性父母对儿童 疫苗接种(aOR:7.22;95% CI:1.64-43.2;p = 0.015)和其他工人(aOR:8.28;95% CI:1.19-48;p = 0.008)的决策相关。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为,尽管 麻疹 的行政覆盖率很高,但 麻疹 仍在继续流通,这凸显了家长和社区决策在 疫苗 接受度中的作用。新颖之处在于识别活动后环境中与 麻疹 IgM 阳性相关的特定社会行为因素。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,加强免疫服务质量、扩大对未接种疫苗的儿童的覆盖范围以及让男性家长和其他社区工作者参与可能会提高 疫苗 的接受度并减少运动后期的 麻疹 传播。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横截面设计限制了因果推断,并且样本量较小可能会降低效应估计的精度。由于详尽的名单登记和未测量的社会行为因素的混杂因素可能导致选择偏差,证据是中等的。

Clinical and epidemiological profile of suspected and laboratory-confirmed measles cases in South Kivu, Democratic Republic of the Congo, 2023

Authors: Matundanya Assumani Joseph, Mulume Musafiri Eric, Bashizi Cito, Christian Ciribagula, Jonathan Tshongo, Kiza Mulambe Daddy, Ngaboyeka Gaylord, Masimango Imani Mannix, Pacifique Mwene-Batu, Lupande Mwenebitu David
Original abstract (English)
Measles remains a major public health challenge in many low-resource settings, despite repeated vaccination efforts. In 2023, a resurgence of suspected measles cases was reported in South Kivu Province (Democratic Republic of Congo) following a mass vaccination campaign targeting children aged 6–59 months. This study aimed to describe the clinical and epidemiological characteristics of suspected measles cases and to identify factors associated with measles IgM positivity in the post-campaign period. A cross-sectional study was conducted among children aged 6–59 months presenting with clinical signs of measles in five health zones most affected by the outbreak (Kalehe, Minova, Idjwi, Miti-Murhesa, and Kalonge). Suspected cases were enrolled exhaustively from line lists provided by local health authorities. After obtaining parental consent, blood samples were collected and tested for measles and rubella IgM antibodies at the National Institute for Biomedical Research (INRB). Data were analyzed using R version 4.3.3. Logistic regression was used to identify characteristics associated with measles IgM positivity among suspected cases. Of the 82 children tested, 39 (48%) were positive for measles IgM. Among the 43 IgM-negative children, 8 (18%) were rubella IgM positive. In adjusted analyses, measles IgM positivity showed an evidence of an association with male parental decision-making regarding child vaccination (aOR :7.22; 95% CI: 1.64–43.2; p = 0.015) and others workers (aOR: 8.28; 95%CI:1.19-48; p = < 0.008). Despite reported high administrative vaccination coverage, measles continues to circulate among children in South Kivu. Strengthening immunization service quality, improving outreach to unvaccinated children, and engaging male parents and others workers may enhance vaccine acceptance and reduce measles transmission in the post-campaign period.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles continues to pose a public health burden in low-resource settings despite vaccination campaigns, creating uncertainty about post-campaign transmission dynamics and determinants of infection. The study addressed a knowledge gap on the clinical and epidemiological profile of suspected measles cases and factors associated with laboratory confirmation in South Kivu Province, Democratic Republic of the Congo.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted in five health zones of South Kivu Province (Kalehe, Minova, Idjwi, Miti-Murhesa, and Kalonge) among children aged 6–59 months presenting with clinical signs of measles. Suspected cases were exhaustively enrolled from line lists provided by local health authorities.
Core methods
Blood samples were collected after obtaining parental consent and tested for measles and rubella IgM antibodies at the National Institute for Biomedical Research (INRB). Data were analyzed using R version 4.3.3, and logistic regression was used to identify characteristics associated with measles IgM positivity among suspected cases.
Main results
Of the 82 children tested, 39 (48%) were positive for measles IgM. Among the 43 IgM-negative children, 8 (18%) were rubella IgM positive. In adjusted analyses, measles IgM positivity showed an association with male parental decision-making regarding child vaccination (aOR: 7.22; 95% CI: 1.64–43.2; p = 0.015) and other workers (aOR: 8.28; 95% CI: 1.19–48; p = 0.008).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating ongoing measles circulation despite high administrative vaccination coverage, highlighting the role of parental and community decision-making in vaccine acceptance. The novelty lies in identifying specific socio-behavioral factors associated with measles IgM positivity in a post-campaign setting.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that strengthening immunization service quality, improving outreach to unvaccinated children, and engaging male parents and other community workers may enhance vaccine acceptance and reduce measles transmission in the post-campaign period.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s cross-sectional design limits causal inference, and the small sample size may reduce precision of effect estimates. The evidence is moderate due to potential selection bias from exhaustive line-list enrollment and unmeasured confounding in socio-behavioral factors.

怀孕期间 麻疹 免疫力的普遍筛查:成本效益分析。

Universal screening for measles immunity during pregnancy: a cost-effectiveness analysis.
作者： Anuradha Devabhaktuni, Sarah Boudova, Elias Kassir, Sohum Shah, Neil S Silverman, Christina S Han
摘要中文翻译背景:麻疹 是一种高度传染性疾病,能够导致显着的发病率和 死亡率,包括不良妊娠结局。目前不建议在怀孕期间对 麻疹 免疫力进行常规筛查。鉴于 麻疹 麻疹 在全国和全球范围内的频率不断增加,对 麻疹 免疫状态的认识可以影响对 麻疹 暴露的孕妇的管理,并为产后或孕前 麻疹 提供机会。目的:我们试图评估美国妊娠期间 麻疹 免疫力普遍筛查的成本效益。研究设计:构建决策分析模型来评估妊娠期间 麻疹 免疫通用筛查与常规护理(即不筛查 麻疹 免疫)相比的成本效益。决策分析的结果是孕妇的质量调整生命年(QALY)。模型输入来自文献。我们的模型运行了两个怀孕周期,每个 QALY 的支付意愿门槛为 100,000 美元。总成本和 QALY 用于衡量普遍 麻疹 筛查与常规护理相比的增量成本效益比 (ICER)。进行敏感性分析以评估结果的稳健性。结果:麻疹 免疫的普遍筛查是主导策略。使用 0.1% 的 麻疹 暴露率来反映最高风险区域,普遍筛查与不筛查的 ICER 为 166.46 美元/QALY。龙卷风敏感性分析表明,麻疹 筛查的成本和 麻疹 暴露的概率对筛查策略的成本效益影响最大。当 麻疹 暴露率从 0.001-0.1% 变化时,筛查仍然是主导策略。在蒙特卡罗模拟过程中,麻疹 孕期免疫筛查不仅节省了成本,而且在 99.83% 的模拟中提高了 QALY。结论:妊娠期间对 麻疹 免疫力进行普遍筛查是一种具有成本效益的策略。我们主张在美国在怀孕期间采用普遍的 麻疹 筛查。在采用之前,提供者应考虑筛查其怀孕患者并推荐产后 疫苗接种。如果实施,将需要进一步研究来评估普遍筛查的实际益处。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
麻疹 是一种高度传染性疾病,能够导致严重的发病率和 死亡率,包括不良妊娠结局。目前不建议在怀孕期间对 麻疹 免疫力进行常规筛查。
设计与对象
构建决策分析模型来评估妊娠期间 麻疹 免疫通用筛查与常规护理(即不筛查 麻疹 免疫)相比的成本效益。该模型运行了两个怀孕周期,每个 QALY 的支付意愿门槛为 100,000 美元。
核心方法
模型输入来自文献。
主要结果
麻疹 免疫的普遍筛查是主导策略。使用 0.1% 的 麻疹 暴露率来反映最高风险区域,普遍筛查与不筛查的 ICER 为 166.46 美元/QALY。当 麻疹 暴露率从 0.001-0.1% 变化时,筛查仍然是主导策略。在蒙特卡罗模拟过程中,麻疹 孕期免疫筛查不仅节省了成本,而且在 99.83% 的模拟中提高了 QALY。
解释与创新
怀孕期间对 麻疹 免疫力进行普遍筛查是一种具有成本效益的策略。研究结果表明,筛查策略的成本效益在各种暴露率范围内都是稳健的。
科研与公卫意义
怀孕期间对 麻疹 免疫力的普遍筛查可以通过为产后或孕前 麻疹 提供机会来为公共卫生政策提供信息。它还可能影响对 麻疹 暴露的孕妇的管理。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于来自文献的模型输入,不包括现实世界的数据。由于依赖假设和间接数据源,证据强度中等。

Universal screening for measles immunity during pregnancy: a cost-effectiveness analysis.

Authors: Anuradha Devabhaktuni, Sarah Boudova, Elias Kassir, Sohum Shah, Neil S Silverman, Christina S Han
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Measles is a highly contagious illness capable of causing significant morbidity and mortality, including adverse pregnancy outcomes. Routine screening for measles immunity is not currently recommended during pregnancy. Given the increasing frequency of measles outbreaks nationally and globally, awareness of measles immunity status can influence management of pregnant individuals who incur measles exposures, and offer an opportunity for postpartum or preconception vaccination. OBJECTIVES: We sought to evaluate the cost-effectiveness of universal screening for measles immunity during pregnancy in the United States. STUDY DESIGN: A decision-analytic model was constructed to evaluate the cost-effectiveness of universal screening for measles immunity during pregnancy compared to usual care (ie, no screening for measles immunity). The outcome of the decision analysis was quality-adjusted life years (QALYs) of the pregnant person. Model inputs were derived from the literature. Our model ran over two pregnancy cycles with a willingness-to-pay threshold of $100,000 per QALY. Total costs and QALYs were used to measure the incremental cost-effectiveness ratio (ICER) of universal measles screening compared with usual care. Sensitivity analyses were conducted to evaluate the robustness of the results. RESULTS: Universal screening for measles immunity was the dominant strategy. Using a measles exposure rate of 0.1% to reflect the highest risk areas, the ICER of universal screening versus no screening was $166.46/QALY. Tornado sensitivity analyses demonstrated that the cost of measles screening and the probability of measles exposure had greatest impact on the cost-effectiveness of the screening strategy. When varying the measles exposure rate from 0.001-0.1%, screening remained the dominant strategy. During Monte Carlo simulation, measles screening for immunity during pregnancy was cost-saving and increased QALYs in 99.83% of simulations. CONCLUSIONS: Universal screening for measles immunity during pregnancy is a cost-effective strategy. We argue for adoption of universal measles screening during pregnancy in the United States. Until adopted, providers should consider screening their pregnant patients and recommending postpartum vaccination. If implemented, further study will be needed to assess the actual benefit of universal screening.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles is a highly contagious illness capable of causing significant morbidity and mortality, including adverse pregnancy outcomes. Routine screening for measles immunity is not currently recommended during pregnancy.
Design and population
A decision-analytic model was constructed to evaluate the cost-effectiveness of universal screening for measles immunity during pregnancy compared to usual care (ie, no screening for measles immunity). The model ran over two pregnancy cycles with a willingness-to-pay threshold of $100,000 per QALY.
Core methods
Model inputs were derived from the literature.
Main results
Universal screening for measles immunity was the dominant strategy. Using a measles exposure rate of 0.1% to reflect the highest risk areas, the ICER of universal screening versus no screening was $166.46/QALY. When varying the measles exposure rate from 0.001-0.1%, screening remained the dominant strategy. During Monte Carlo simulation, measles screening for immunity during pregnancy was cost-saving and increased QALYs in 99.83% of simulations.
Interpretation and novelty
Universal screening for measles immunity during pregnancy is a cost-effective strategy. The findings demonstrate that the cost-effectiveness of the screening strategy is robust across a range of exposure rates.
Scientific and public-health significance
Universal screening for measles immunity during pregnancy can inform public health policy by offering an opportunity for postpartum or preconception vaccination. It may also influence management of pregnant individuals who incur measles exposures.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on model inputs derived from the literature and does not include real-world data. The evidence strength is moderate due to reliance on assumptions and indirect data sources.

描述 2025 年西德克萨斯州 麻疹 期间美国退伍军人群体中 麻疹 IgG 相关 血清流行率 的特征。

Characterizing Measles IgG-related Seroprevalence in the US Veteran Population during the 2025 West Texas outbreak.
作者： Chris A Gentry, George Kurdgelashvili, Robert H Scofield
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
麻疹 在美国的复苏引发了人们对人群免疫力差距的担忧。该研究解决了 2025 年 麻疹 IgG 血清阴性率和美国退伍军人群体检测模式方面的知识差距。
设计与对象
使用美国退伍军人健康管理局 (VHA) 数据库进行了一项全国性回顾性观察研究。包括 2024 年(麻疹 之前)和 2025 年(暴发)1 月至 6 月进行过 麻疹 IgG 抗体测试的退伍军人。
核心方法
在 麻疹 之前和 暴发 期间收集 麻疹 IgG 测试和血清状态的数据。使用统计模型确定与血清阴性相关的自变量。
主要结果
麻疹 IgG 检测在 麻疹 期间增加了 60.3%(检测了 16,446 名退伍军人)。非 暴发 期间的血清阴性率为 11.9%,暴发 期间的血清阴性率为 9.18%(p<0.0001)。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为确定了感染和 传播 风险较高的人群,特别是那些免疫功能低下的人群。他们强调需要对这些群体进行有针对性的干预措施。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为退伍军人(尤其是患有免疫缺陷或使用 Janus 激酶抑制剂的退伍军人)提供 麻疹 策略和有针对性的血清学筛查信息,以减轻 麻疹 卷土重来的风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究的回顾性设计和对现有 VHA 数据的依赖可能会限制普遍性并引入选择偏差。由于这些限制,证据强度被评估为中等。

Characterizing Measles IgG-related Seroprevalence in the US Veteran Population during the 2025 West Texas outbreak.

Authors: Chris A Gentry, George Kurdgelashvili, Robert H Scofield
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: The 2025 United States (US) measles outbreak supports concerns that measles may have significant resurgences. The study's objectives were to characterize measles serologic testing rates, estimate measles IgG seronegativity rates, and determine independent variables associated with seronegativity across the US Veterans Health Administration (VHA). METHODS: A nationwide retrospective, observational study was conducted collecting data from veterans with measles IgG antibody testing performed from January through June in 2024 (pre-outbreak) and 2025 (outbreak). Endpoints included determining independent variables associated with 1) measles IgG testing within the outbreak period versus the pre-outbreak period, and 2) measles IgG seronegativity within the outbreak period. RESULTS: 16,446 veterans had measles IgG testing in the outbreak period, a 60.3% increase in the testing rate compared to the non-outbreak period. Seronegativity was found in 1,220 (11.9%) veterans in the non-outbreak period and 1,509 veterans (9.18%) in the outbreak period (p<0.0001). Independent variables associated with seronegativity included age 64 years or below (OR 5.65, 95% CI 4.59-6.94), Janus kinase inhibitor use (OR 3.54, CI 1.24-10.1), and immunodeficient condition (OR 3.10, CI 2.34-4.11). CONCLUSIONS: As measles reemerges, certain populations were identified that may be at higher risk of infection and transmission, particularly those with immune compromise.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles resurgence in the US raised concerns about population immunity gaps. The study addressed knowledge gaps on measles IgG seronegativity rates and testing patterns in the US veteran population during the 2025 outbreak.
Design and population
A nationwide retrospective observational study was conducted using the US Veterans Health Administration (VHA) database. Veterans with measles IgG antibody testing performed from January through June in 2024 (pre-outbreak) and 2025 (outbreak) were included.
Core methods
Data were collected on measles IgG testing and serostatus during pre-outbreak and outbreak periods. Independent variables associated with seronegativity were determined using statistical models.
Main results
Measles IgG testing increased by 60.3% during the outbreak period (16,446 veterans tested). Seronegativity was 11.9% in the non-outbreak period and 9.18% in the outbreak period (p<0.0001).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as identifying populations at higher risk of infection and transmission, particularly those with immune compromise. They highlighted the need for targeted interventions in these groups.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform vaccination strategies and targeted serologic screening in veterans, especially those with immunodeficiency or on Janus kinase inhibitors, to mitigate measles resurgence risks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's retrospective design and reliance on existing VHA data may limit generalizability and introduce selection bias. The evidence strength is assessed as moderate due to these limitations.

麻疹 2024 年在波兰

Measles in Poland in 2024
作者： Karolina Mrozowska-Nyckowska, Jakub Maciej Zbrzeżniak, Iwona Paradowska-Stankiewicz
摘要中文翻译背景 麻疹 仍然是最具传染性的病毒性疾病之一,在 WHO 欧洲区域消除该疾病需要维持高 疫苗接种 发生率和有效的流行病学 监测。COVID-19 大流行后,包括波兰在内的许多欧洲国家的病例数量再次增加。目的 本研究的目的是评估 2024 年波兰 麻疹 的流行病学状况,同时考虑 麻疹 人群的 麻疹 状况以及世界卫生组织 (WHO) 麻疹 根除计划在波兰的实施程度。材料和方法 使用国家传染病 麻疹 系统获得的数据评估波兰 2024 年 麻疹 的流行病学状况,数据由 Epibaza 系统中当地卫生流行病学站记录。更多信息来自 2015-2024 年年度公报“波兰的传染病和中毒”和“波兰的 疫苗接种”。结果 2024 年,波兰记录的 麻疹 病例数量有所增加。总共报告了 272 例病例(发病率 发病率为 0.72/100,000),而 2023 年为 35 例(0.09/100,000),增加了七倍多,这主要是由于上一年的病例数非常低。报告病例的增加与几个 麻疹 暴发 有关,主要发生在马佐夫舍省和下西拉斯省。总共识别出 23 个 暴发。与 COVID-19 之前时期(2019 年)相关的分析表明,发病率 率持续较低。2019年报告病例1,502例(3.31/10万)。与2018-2022年的中位数相比,2024年的病例数增加了八倍多。发病率 最高的是 0-9 岁儿童。病例和住院人数的增加与 疫苗接种 覆盖率下降至 95% 以下同时发生,增加了当地 疫苗接种 的风险。结论 在波兰保持 麻疹 消除需要使用两剂 麻疹 维持至少 95% 的覆盖率,腮腺炎和风疹 (MMR) 疫苗。这应该通过持续的常规免疫、易感个体的追赶疫苗接种以及进一步加强流行病学和实验室监测(包括循环麻疹病毒的分子特征)来支持。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
麻疹 仍然是最具传染性的病毒性疾病之一,在世卫组织欧洲区域消除该病需要保持高 疫苗接种 发生率和有效的流行病学 监测。
设计与对象
该研究利用Epibaza系统当地卫生流行病学站记录的国家传染病麻疹系统的数据评估了2024年波兰麻疹的流行病学情况。
核心方法
材料和方法包括从国家传染病 监测 系统获得的数据、由 Epibaza 系统中当地卫生和流行病学站记录的数据,以及 2015-2024 年年度公报“波兰传染病和中毒”和“波兰 疫苗接种”的附加信息。
主要结果
2024年,波兰报告的麻疹病例数量增加,共报告272例(发病率发病率为0.72/100,000),而2023年仅报告35例(0.09/100,000),较前一年增长超过7倍,主要由于前一年病例数量极低。
解释与创新
报告病例的增加与几个 麻疹 暴发 有关,主要发生在马佐夫舍省和下西拉斯省,与 COVID-19 之前时期(2019 年)相关的分析表明,发病率 率持续较低。
科研与公卫意义
病例和住院人数的增加与 疫苗接种 覆盖率下降至 95% 以下同时发生,增加了当地 疫苗接种 的风险,并凸显了维持高 疫苗接种 率以防止复发的必要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于国家监测系统和年度公报的数据,这些数据在完整性或准确性方面可能存在局限性,并且由于依赖于报告的数据而不是直接的实验室确认,证据不充分。

Measles in Poland in 2024

Authors: Karolina Mrozowska-Nyckowska, Jakub Maciej Zbrzeżniak, Iwona Paradowska-Stankiewicz
Original abstract (English)
Background Measles remains one of the most contagious viral diseases, and its elimination in the WHO European Region requires maintaining high vaccination rates and effective epidemiological surveillance. Following the COVID-19 pandemic, many European countries, including Poland, are seeing a renewed increase in the number of cases. Objective The aim of the study was to assess the epidemiological situation of measles in Poland in 2024, taking into account the vaccination status of the population against measles and the degree of implementation of the World Health Organization (WHO) measles eradication program in Poland. Material and methods The epidemiological situation of measles in Poland in 2024 was assessed using data obtained from the national infectious disease surveillance system, recorded by the local sanitary and epidemiological stations in the Epibaza system. Additional information was obtained from the annual bulletins “Infectious Diseases and Poisonings in Poland” and “Vaccinations in Poland” for the years 2015-2024. Results In 2024, an increase in the number of measles cases was recorded in Poland. A total of 272 cases were reported (incidence rate 0.72/100,000), compared with 35 cases in 2023 (0.09/100,000), representing representing more than a sevenfold increase, largely due to the very low number of cases in the previous year. The rise in reported cases was associated with several measles outbreaks, primarily in the Mazowieckie and Dolnośląskie voivodeships. Altogether, 23 outbreaks were identified. Analysis in relation to the pre-COVID-19 period (2019) indicates a persistently lower incidence rate. In 2019, 1,502 cases were reported (3.31/100,000). Compared with the median for 2018-2022, the number of cases in 2024 was more than eight times higher. The highest incidence was observed among children aged 0-9 years. The increase in cases and hospitalizations coincided with a decline in vaccination coverage below 95%, increasing the risk of local outbreaks. Conclusions Maintaining measles elimination in Poland requires sustaining at least 95% coverage with two doses of the measles, mumps and rubella (MMR) vaccine. This should be supported by continued routine immunization, catch-up vaccination of susceptible individuals, and further strengthening of epidemiological and laboratory surveillance, including molecular characterization of circulating measles viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles remains one of the most contagious viral diseases, and its elimination in the WHO European Region requires maintaining high vaccination rates and effective epidemiological surveillance.
Design and population
The study assessed the epidemiological situation of measles in Poland in 2024 using data from the national infectious disease surveillance system, recorded by local sanitary and epidemiological stations in the Epibaza system.
Core methods
Material and methods included data obtained from the national infectious disease surveillance system, recorded by the local sanitary and epidemiological stations in the Epibaza system, and additional information from the annual bulletins “Infectious Diseases and Poisonings in Poland” and “Vaccinations in Poland” for the years 2015-2024.
Main results
In 2024, an increase in the number of measles cases was recorded in Poland, with a total of 272 cases reported (incidence rate 0.72/100,000), compared with 35 cases in 2023 (0.09/100,000), representing more than a sevenfold increase, largely due to the very low number of cases in the previous year.
Interpretation and novelty
The rise in reported cases was associated with several measles outbreaks, primarily in the Mazowieckie and Dolnośląskie voivodeships, and analysis in relation to the pre-COVID-19 period (2019) indicates a persistently lower incidence rate.
Scientific and public-health significance
The increase in cases and hospitalizations coincided with a decline in vaccination coverage below 95%, increasing the risk of local outbreaks, and highlights the need for maintaining high vaccination rates to prevent resurgence.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on data from the national surveillance system and annual bulletins, which may have limitations in completeness or accuracy, and the evidence is moderate due to the reliance on reported data rather than direct laboratory confirmation.

具有不同易感性和传染性的离散状态结构流行病模型及其在美国 麻疹 暴发 中的应用

Discrete state-structured epidemic model with varying susceptibility and infectivity and its application to the measles outbreak in the United States
作者： Wenxuan Li, Chiyu Zhang, Xu Chen, Guyue Liu, Suli Liu
摘要中文翻译患有传染病 宿主 的个体经常在不同的易感性和传染性状态之间转变。因此,我们推导出一类流行病模型,其中易感者和感染者分别具有一组离散的易感性和感染性状态。我们的模型基于双剂量 疫苗接种,并考虑了状态之间所有可能的状态转换。对所提出的模型进行了全面的数学分析,包括评估控制繁殖数和解决方案的全局动态行为。通过使用改进的仿射不变集成马尔可夫链蒙特卡罗方法,我们还对美国麻疹案例数据进行了拟合。结果表明,尽管疫苗接种覆盖率和有效率较高,但零星或小规模的麻疹 暴发仍然存在。这表明仅依赖疫苗接种政策不足以完全中断其传播链。因此,在现有 麻疹 工作的基础上,提高 麻疹 案件的 麻疹 率是控制 麻疹、传播 和 暴发 的另一项关键措施。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解释了尽管 麻疹 覆盖率和功效较高,但 麻疹 暴发 仍然存在的原因,确定了离散易感性和传染性状态如何影响 传播 动态方面的知识差距。
设计与对象
作者开发了一种离散状态结构的流行病模型,其中包含不同的易感性和传染性状态,并将其应用于美国的 麻疹 病例数据。
核心方法
使用改进的仿射不变系综马尔可夫链蒙特卡罗方法将模型拟合到麻疹案例数据,并进行数学分析,包括控制再生数和解的全局动态行为。
主要结果
结果表明,尽管 疫苗接种 覆盖率和有效率较高,但零星或小规模的 麻疹 暴发 仍然存在。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明仅靠 疫苗接种 政策不足以完全中断 麻疹 传播 链,强调需要提高 诊断 费率作为补充控制措施。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,提高 诊断 率和 疫苗接种 可以加强 麻疹 控制工作,特别是在覆盖率高但持续存在 传播 的环境中。
局限与证据强度
证据受到对模型拟合案例数据的限制,没有报告针对独立数据集或实验对照的验证,需要进行适度的置信度评估。

Discrete state-structured epidemic model with varying susceptibility and infectivity and its application to the measles outbreak in the United States

Authors: Wenxuan Li, Chiyu Zhang, Xu Chen, Guyue Liu, Suli Liu
Original abstract (English)
Host individuals with vaccinations for infectious diseases often transition among different states of susceptibility and infectivity. Thus, we derive a class of epidemic models in which susceptible and infected individuals have a discrete set of susceptibility and infectivity states, respectively. Our model is based on double-dose vaccination and accounts for all possible state transitions between states. A comprehensive mathematical analysis of the proposed model is conducted, including assessing the control reproduction number and the global dynamical behaviors of the solutions. By using an improved affine-invariant ensemble Markov chain Monte Carlo method, we also fit the model with measles case data in the United States. The results indicate that despite high vaccination coverage and efficacy rates, sporadic or small-scale measles outbreaks still occur. This suggests that reliance solely on vaccination policies is insufficient to fully interrupt its transmission chain. Consequently, enhancing the diagnosis rate of measles cases is another crucial measure for controlling measles transmission and outbreaks, building upon existing vaccination efforts.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses why measles outbreaks persist despite high vaccination coverage and efficacy, identifying a knowledge gap in how discrete susceptibility and infectivity states influence transmission dynamics.
Design and population
The authors developed a discrete state-structured epidemic model incorporating varying susceptibility and infectivity states, applied to measles case data in the United States.
Core methods
The model was fitted to measles case data using an improved affine-invariant ensemble Markov chain Monte Carlo method, with mathematical analysis including the control reproduction number and global dynamical behaviors of solutions.
Main results
The results indicate that sporadic or small-scale measles outbreaks still occur despite high vaccination coverage and efficacy rates.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as evidence that vaccination policies alone are insufficient to fully interrupt measles transmission chains, highlighting the need for enhanced diagnosis rates as a complementary control measure.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that improving diagnosis rates alongside vaccination could strengthen measles control efforts, particularly in settings with high coverage but persistent transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by reliance on model fitting to case data without reported validation against independent datasets or experimental controls, warranting a moderate confidence assessment.

量化 麻疹 麻疹 中延时报告和支持性护理的影响:美国德克萨斯州的模拟方法。

Quantifying the impact of time-delayed reporting and supportive care in measles outbreak: A simulation approach for Texas, USA.
作者： Chidozie Williams Chukwu, Ousmane Koutou, Moussa Ouédraogo, Dawit Denu
摘要中文翻译本研究探讨了最近在美国德克萨斯州发生的 麻疹 期间,疫苗接种 反应、病例报告和获得支持性护理的时间延迟如何影响 麻疹 传播。我们开发了一种基于延迟的区室模型,该模型捕获了 麻疹 控制的生物学和操作特征,并使用德克萨斯州报告的 2025 年 1 月 20 日至 5 月 27 日的 麻疹 发病率 数据对其进行校准。分析和数值结果表明,报告和干预的延迟会大大削弱 麻疹 控制,使 传播 即使在高度接种疫苗的情况下也能持续存在人口。相比之下,及时发现病例、提高 疫苗接种 的吸收率以及获得支持性护理可显着减少 传播 的数量,并可使有效繁殖数低于统一值。这些发现强调了最大限度地减少报告和医疗延误以及持续免疫工作对于防止 麻疹 卷土重来并支持德克萨斯州和类似地区消除 麻疹 目标的关键作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了为什么尽管 疫苗接种 覆盖率很高,但在美国德克萨斯州 麻疹 暴发 期间 疫苗接种 响应、病例报告和支持性护理的延迟会破坏控制工作并威胁消除目标。
设计与对象
作者开发了一种基于延迟的分区模型,使用 2025 年 1 月 20 日至 5 月 27 日德克萨斯州 麻疹 发病率 数据进行校准,以研究运行延迟如何影响 传播 动态。
核心方法
该研究使用了结合了 麻疹 控制的生物学和操作特征的区室模型,并根据指定时期报告的 麻疹 发病率 数据进行了校准。
主要结果
分析发现,报告和干预的延迟可能导致 麻疹 传播 即使在高度接种疫苗的人群中也能持续存在,而及时检测、改善 疫苗接种 的吸收和支持性护理会减少 麻疹 并导致有效繁殖数低于统一值。
解释与创新
研究结果强调,最大限度地减少报告和医疗延误以及持续免疫对于防止 麻疹 卷土重来并支持德克萨斯州和类似地区的消除目标至关重要。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,公共卫生策略必须优先考虑快速病例检测、疫苗接种 响应和支持性护理,以有效控制 麻疹 麻疹 并维持消除目标。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于德克萨斯州单个 暴发 时期校准的分区模型,这可能会限制对其他地区或 暴发 背景的普遍适用性,并且由于仅抽象数据源,证据强度被评估为中等。

Quantifying the impact of time-delayed reporting and supportive care in measles outbreak: A simulation approach for Texas, USA.

Authors: Chidozie Williams Chukwu, Ousmane Koutou, Moussa Ouédraogo, Dawit Denu
Original abstract (English)
This study examines how time delays in vaccination response, case reporting, and access to supportive care influence measles transmission during the recent outbreak in Texas, USA. We develop a delay-based compartmental model that captures both biological and operational features of measles control and calibrate it using Texas reported Measles incidence data from January 20 to May 27, 2025. Analytical and numerical results show that delays in reporting and intervention can substantially weaken outbreak control, allowing transmission to persist even in highly vaccinated populations. In contrast, timely case detection, improved vaccination uptake, and access to supportive care significantly reduce transmission and can drive the effective reproduction number below unity. These findings highlight the critical role of minimizing reporting and healthcare delays alongside sustained immunization efforts to prevent measles resurgence and support elimination goals in Texas and similar settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses why delays in vaccination response, case reporting, and supportive care during a measles outbreak in Texas, USA, undermine control efforts and threaten elimination goals despite high vaccination coverage.
Design and population
The authors developed a delay-based compartmental model calibrated with Texas measles incidence data from January 20 to May 27, 2025, to examine how operational delays influence transmission dynamics.
Core methods
The study used a compartmental model incorporating biological and operational features of measles control, with calibration performed against reported measles incidence data from the specified period.
Main results
The analysis found that delays in reporting and intervention can allow measles transmission to persist even in highly vaccinated populations, whereas timely detection, improved vaccination uptake, and supportive care reduce transmission and can drive the effective reproduction number below unity.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings emphasize that minimizing reporting and healthcare delays, alongside sustained immunization, is critical to preventing measles resurgence and supporting elimination goals in Texas and similar settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that public health strategies must prioritize rapid case detection, vaccination response, and supportive care access to effectively control measles outbreaks and maintain elimination targets.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on a compartmental model calibrated to a single outbreak period in Texas, which may limit generalizability to other regions or outbreak contexts, and the evidence strength is assessed as moderate due to the abstract-only data source.

麻疹病毒 感染会在人源化免疫系统小鼠模型中诱发白细胞减少症和皮疹

Infection with measles virus induces leukopenia and rash in a humanized immune system mouse model
作者： Tomoko Nakanishi, Misako Yoneda, Tomoko Fujiyuki, Yosuke Amagai, Izumu Saito, Chieko Kai
摘要中文翻译摘要 人类是 麻疹病毒 (MV) 的天然 麻疹病毒。除非人类灵长类动物外,没有其他动物物种天然易受 MV 感染。因此,不存在用于研究 MV 致病机制的小动物模型。本研究的目的是使用植入人脐带血来源的 CD34 + 细胞的 NOD/LtSz-scid/IL2Rγ null (NSG) 免疫缺陷小鼠建立 MV 感染的小动物模型,并评估其作为 麻疹 模型的适用性。用表达增强型绿色荧光蛋白(EGFP)的重组野生型MV株感染人源化小鼠后,在脾脏、淋巴结和骨髓中检测到强烈的EGFP信号。这种荧光被证实与人类 T 和 B 细胞中的 MV 感染有关。此外,小鼠表现出白细胞减少症和皮疹样绿色荧光病灶,让人想起 麻疹 皮疹。这些病灶起源于毛囊附近人类白细胞的积累,与非人类灵长类动物模型中观察到的病理学一致。这些结果表明,这种小动物模型可能是阐明 MV 感染机制及其致病性的有价值的工具。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
麻疹病毒 (MV) 是一种人类病原体,没有天然易感的小动物模型,限制了其致病性的机制研究。该研究通过询问人源化免疫系统小鼠是否可以重现 MV 感染和疾病特征,解决了缺乏合适动物模型的问题。
设计与对象
该研究使用植入人脐带血来源的 CD34+ 细胞的 NOD/LtSz-scid/IL2Rγ null (NSG) 免疫缺陷小鼠建立了小动物模型来评估 MV 感染。目的是评估该模型对于研究 MV 致病性的适用性。
核心方法
作者用表达增强型绿色荧光蛋白 (EGFP) 的重组野生型 麻疹病毒 菌株感染人源化小鼠,并通过组织中的荧光检测和临床症状评估来评估感染情况。
主要结果
感染后,在脾脏、淋巴结和骨髓中检测到强 EGFP 信号,证实与人类 T 和 B 细胞中的 MV 感染有关。小鼠表现出白细胞减少症和皮疹样绿色荧光病灶,与 麻疹 病理学一致。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为人源化小鼠模型概括了在非人类灵长类动物中观察到的 MV 感染和致病性的关键特征的证据。他们的结论是,该模型为阐明 MV 机制提供了一个有价值的工具。
科研与公卫意义
该模型可以实现 MV 发病机制 的机制研究、药物测试和 疫苗 评估,而这些研究以前由于缺乏小动物模型而无法实现。它还可以促进对 麻疹 相关并发症的研究。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于摘要报告,这限制了详细的方法和结果报告。由于缺乏完整的方法学描述、样本量、统计分析和全面的病理评估,证据的强度较低。

Infection with measles virus induces leukopenia and rash in a humanized immune system mouse model

Authors: Tomoko Nakanishi, Misako Yoneda, Tomoko Fujiyuki, Yosuke Amagai, Izumu Saito, Chieko Kai
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Humans are the natural hosts of the measles virus (MV). With the exception of non-human primates, no other animal species are naturally susceptible to MV infection. Thus, no small animal models exist to study the mechanisms of MV pathogenicity. The aim of the present study was to establish a small animal model of MV infection using NOD/LtSz-scid/IL2Rγ null (NSG) immunodeficient mice implanted with human-cord-blood-derived CD34 + cells and to evaluate its suitability as a measles model. Upon infecting the humanized mice with a recombinant wild-type strain MV expressing enhanced green fluorescent protein (EGFP), a strong EGFP signal was detected in the spleen, lymph nodes, and bone marrow. This fluorescence was confirmed to be associated with MV infection in human T and B cells. Furthermore, the mice exhibited leukopenia and rash-like green fluorescent foci reminiscent of a measles rash. These foci originated from the accumulation of human leukocytes adjacent to hair follicles, consistent with the pathology observed in non-human primate models. These results suggest that this small animal model could be a valuable tool for elucidating the mechanisms of MV infection and its pathogenicity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles virus (MV) is a human pathogen with no naturally susceptible small animal model, limiting mechanistic studies of its pathogenicity. The study addressed the lack of suitable animal models by asking whether a humanized immune system mouse could recapitulate MV infection and disease features.
Design and population
The study established a small animal model using NOD/LtSz-scid/IL2Rγ null (NSG) immunodeficient mice implanted with human-cord-blood-derived CD34+ cells to evaluate MV infection. The aim was to assess the suitability of this model for studying MV pathogenicity.
Core methods
The authors infected humanized mice with a recombinant wild-type measles virus strain expressing enhanced green fluorescent protein (EGFP) and evaluated infection through fluorescence detection in tissues and assessment of clinical signs.
Main results
Upon infection, strong EGFP signals were detected in the spleen, lymph nodes, and bone marrow, confirmed to be associated with MV infection in human T and B cells. The mice exhibited leukopenia and rash-like green fluorescent foci consistent with measles pathology.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence that the humanized mouse model recapitulates key features of MV infection and pathogenicity observed in non-human primates. They concluded that this model provides a valuable tool for elucidating MV mechanisms.
Scientific and public-health significance
This model may enable mechanistic studies of MV pathogenesis, drug testing, and vaccine evaluation that were previously infeasible due to the lack of small animal models. It could also facilitate research into measles-associated complications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to an abstract-only report, which restricts detailed methodological and result reporting. The strength of the evidence is low due to the absence of full methodological descriptions, sample sizes, statistical analyses, and comprehensive pathological assessments.

2026 年孟加拉国 麻疹 暴发:对免疫差距和护理缺陷的批评

The 2026 Measles Outbreak in Bangladesh: A Critique of Immunity Gaps and Care Deficiencies
作者： Syed Abu Hasanth
摘要中文翻译孟加拉国在 5 月中旬达到顶峰的严重 麻疹 麻疹 中进入 2026 年。本文分析了公共卫生部门对 暴发 的反应,并评估了政府对危机的管理。卫生专业人员和 麻疹 专家将 麻疹 归因于常规免疫积累的差距、公共卫生基础设施薄弱、儿童营养状况不佳、维生素 A 普遍缺乏以及孕产妇在怀孕和哺乳期间营养不良。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
孟加拉国进入2026年,正值一场严重的麻疹 麻疹 麻疹 麻疹 麻疹在5月中旬达到顶峰,促使人们对公共卫生应对措施和政府对危机的管理进行分析。
设计与对象
该研究评估了孟加拉国对2026年麻疹 暴发的公共卫生应对措施,重点关注政府对危机的管理。
核心方法
卫生专业人员和 麻疹 专家将 麻疹 归因于常规免疫积累的差距、公共卫生基础设施薄弱、儿童营养状况不佳、维生素 A 普遍缺乏以及孕产妇在怀孕和哺乳期间营养不良。
主要结果
该研究确定了常规免疫接种中累积的差距、公共卫生基础设施的薄弱、儿童营养状况不佳、维生素 A 普遍缺乏以及孕产妇在怀孕和母乳喂养期间营养不良是导致 暴发 的因素。
解释与创新
作者将 麻疹 解释为免疫和公共卫生基础设施系统性失败的结果,强调了孟加拉国背景下营养缺乏和 麻疹 脆弱性的新交叉。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明需要加强常规免疫规划、改善公共卫生基础设施和有针对性的营养干预措施,以预防未来的 麻疹 暴发。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究依赖于专家归因,没有主要数据收集或统计分析,限制了因果推断。

The 2026 Measles Outbreak in Bangladesh: A Critique of Immunity Gaps and Care Deficiencies

Authors: Syed Abu Hasanth
Original abstract (English)
Bangladesh entered 2026 amid a severe measles outbreak that peaked in mid-May. This article analyses the public health response to the outbreak and evaluates the government's management of the crisis. Health professionals and measles experts have attributed the outbreak to accumulated gaps in routine immunisation, weaknesses in public health infrastructure, poor nutritional status among children, widespread vitamin A deficiency, and maternal undernutrition during pregnancy and breastfeeding.

Question and background
Bangladesh entered 2026 amid a severe measles outbreak that peaked in mid-May, prompting an analysis of the public health response and government management of the crisis.
Design and population
The study evaluates the public health response to the 2026 measles outbreak in Bangladesh, focusing on the government's management of the crisis.
Core methods
Health professionals and measles experts attributed the outbreak to accumulated gaps in routine immunisation, weaknesses in public health infrastructure, poor nutritional status among children, widespread vitamin A deficiency, and maternal undernutrition during pregnancy and breastfeeding.
Main results
The study identifies accumulated gaps in routine immunisation, weaknesses in public health infrastructure, poor nutritional status among children, widespread vitamin A deficiency, and maternal undernutrition during pregnancy and breastfeeding as contributing factors to the outbreak.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the outbreak as a result of systemic failures in immunisation and public health infrastructure, highlighting the novel intersection of nutritional deficiencies and measles vulnerability in the Bangladeshi context.
Scientific and public-health significance

ECHO:一种轻量级工具,用于根据病原体系统发育推断缺失病例数

ECHO: A lightweight tool for inferring missing case counts from pathogen phylogenies
作者： Renny Doig, Caroline Colijn
摘要中文翻译AO_SCPLOWBSTRACTC_SCPLOW定时系统发育树以时间单位表达病原体 暴发 的进化历史,提供对一组类群的共同祖先的经过时间的估计。通过将经过的时间与有关疾病 流行病学 的已知信息相结合,我们可以将总分支长度与与 系统发育 相关的病例总数联系起来。这提供了与 系统发育 相关的未排序案例数量的信息。我们称这些为“神秘”案例。我们提出了 ECHO(Estimation of Cryptic 宿主 from 暴发 trees),它是 系统发育 中神秘案例数量的三个轻量级估计器的集合。ECHO 与采样过程的形式无关,这使其对各种形式的采样异质性具有鲁棒性。我们通过模拟展示了 ECHO 的基线准确性及其对异构采样框架的鲁棒性。此外,我们将 ECHO 应用于 2021 年在美国 麻疹病毒 期间收集的 麻疹病毒 序列。ECHO 能够在模拟和实践中以合理的准确度恢复神秘病例的数量。我们讨论 ECHO 最适用的环境及其估计的解释。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
病原体的系统发育树提供了进化时间尺度,但不能直接量化与 暴发 相关的未测序病例。该研究解决了估计在流行病学上与测序样本相关但未在 系统发育 中表示的神秘病例数量方面的知识差距。
设计与对象
该研究引入了 ECHO(Estimation of Cryptic 宿主 from 暴发 trees),这是一种由三个轻量级估计器组成的工具,旨在从定时系统发育树中推断缺失病例数。作者通过模拟展示了该工具的性能,并将其应用于 2021 年美国 麻疹病毒 期间收集的 麻疹病毒 序列。
核心方法
ECHO 使用定时系统发育树将总分支长度与病例总数联系起来,从而估计神秘病例。该工具被设计为与采样过程无关,确保异构采样框架的稳健性。使用模拟研究和对现实世界 麻疹病毒 数据的应用来评估准确性。
主要结果
当应用于 2021 USA 麻疹病毒 序列时,ECHO 在模拟和实践中都以合理的准确度恢复了神秘案例的数量。
解释与创新
作者将 ECHO 的估计解释为对 传播 期间神秘的 传播 范围提供了可行的见解。新颖之处在于开发了一种轻量级、与采样无关的工具,专门用于从系统发育数据推断缺失病例数。
科研与公卫意义
ECHO 可以通过量化神秘病例来为 暴发 调查和响应提供信息,从而提高 监测 的准确性并指导针对 麻疹 和具有类似 传播 动态的潜在其他病原体的公共卫生干预措施。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告具体的样本量、统计置信区间或超出工具对采样异质性的不可知论的详细限制。证据强度中等,因为研究结果得到了模拟和单个现实应用的支持。

ECHO: A lightweight tool for inferring missing case counts from pathogen phylogenies

Authors: Renny Doig, Caroline Colijn
Original abstract (English)
AO_SCPLOWBSTRACTC_SCPLOWTimed phylogenetic trees express the evolutionary history of a pathogen outbreak in units of time, providing an estimate of the elapsed time across the shared ancestry of a set of taxa. By combining this elapsed time with known information about the epidemiology of a disease, we can relate the total branch length to the total number of cases related to the phylogeny. This gives information about the number of unsequenced cases that are related to the phylogeny. We call these "cryptic" cases. We present ECHO (Estimation of Cryptic Hosts from Outbreak trees), a collection of three lightweight estimators of the number of cryptic cases in a phylogeny. ECHO is agnostic to the form of the sampling process, making it robust to a variety of forms of sampling heterogeneity. We demonstrate ECHOs baseline accuracy and its robustness to heterogenous sampling frameworks through simulation. Additionally, we apply ECHO to measles virus sequences that were collected during an outbreak in the USA in 2021. ECHO is able to recover the number of cryptic cases with a reasonable degree of accuracy both in simulation and in practice. We discuss the contexts in which ECHO is most applicable, and the interpretation of its estimates.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Phylogenetic trees of pathogens provide evolutionary time scales but do not directly quantify unsequenced cases linked to an outbreak. The study addresses the knowledge gap in estimating the number of cryptic cases that are epidemiologically connected to sequenced samples but not represented in the phylogeny.
Design and population
The study introduces ECHO (Estimation of Cryptic Hosts from Outbreak trees), a tool comprising three lightweight estimators designed to infer missing case counts from timed phylogenetic trees. The authors demonstrate the tool’s performance through simulation and apply it to measles virus sequences collected during a 2021 outbreak in the USA.
Core methods
ECHO uses timed phylogenetic trees to relate total branch length to the total number of cases, thereby estimating cryptic cases. The tool is designed to be agnostic to sampling processes, ensuring robustness to heterogeneous sampling frameworks. Simulation studies and application to real-world measles virus data were used to evaluate accuracy.
Main results
ECHO recovered the number of cryptic cases with a reasonable degree of accuracy both in simulation and in practice when applied to measles virus sequences from the 2021 USA outbreak.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret ECHO’s estimates as providing actionable insights into the extent of cryptic transmission during outbreaks. The novelty lies in the development of a lightweight, sampling-agnostic tool specifically designed to infer missing case counts from phylogenetic data.
Scientific and public-health significance
ECHO can inform outbreak investigation and response by quantifying cryptic cases, thereby improving surveillance accuracy and guiding public health interventions for measles and potentially other pathogens with similar transmission dynamics.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report the specific sample sizes, statistical confidence intervals, or detailed limitations beyond the tool’s agnosticism to sampling heterogeneity. The evidence strength is moderate, as the findings are supported by simulation and a single real-world application.

通过实践社区加强美国卫生部门的 暴发 响应和准备。

Strengthening Outbreak Response and Preparedness in US Health Departments Through a Community of Practice.
作者： Sutyajeet Soneja, Haley Wellham, Sarah Gillani, Fatima Ameaka, Elizabeth A. Campbell, Caitlin Rivers
摘要中文翻译美国 麻疹 等传染病的重新出现给州、地方、部落和地区卫生部门应对和遏制疾病传播带来了巨大压力。卫生部门的行动涵盖了广泛的 暴发 准备和响应举措,一些卫生部门在其方法上表现出色,而另一些卫生部门则在资源有限的情况下致力于能力建设。为了加强 暴发 的准备和响应,约翰霍普金斯大学 暴发 响应创新中心于 2025 年召开了全国实践社区系列活动,汇集了来自 40 个卫生部门的 115 名参与者。演讲强调了跨学科协作、系统适应性和持续学习对于增强 暴发 响应能力的重要性。该系列强调公共卫生实践的可持续改进依赖于通过共享学习网络将学术研究和实际操作联系起来,确保技术创新转化为全国范围内有效且可扩展的疾病应对策略。

主要结果

Strengthening Outbreak Response and Preparedness in US Health Departments Through a Community of Practice.

Authors: Sutyajeet Soneja, Haley Wellham, Sarah Gillani, Fatima Ameaka, Elizabeth A. Campbell, Caitlin Rivers
Original abstract (English)
The reemergence of infectious diseases, such as measles, in the United States has placed significant strain on state, local, tribal, and territorial health departments to respond and contain the spread of disease. The actions by health departments have spanned a wide range of outbreak preparedness and response initiatives, with some health departments excelling in their approaches while others work to build capacity despite resource constraints. To strengthen outbreak preparedness and response, the Johns Hopkins Center for Outbreak Response Innovation convened a national Community of Practice series in 2025, bringing together 115 participants from 40 health departments. Presentations emphasized the importance of interdisciplinary collaboration, system adaptability, and continuous learning to enhance outbreak response capacity. This series underscored that sustainable improvements in public health practice rely on bridging academic research and operational realities through shared learning networks, ensuring that technical innovations translate into effective and scalable disease response strategies nationwide.

Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

苏格兰 COVID-19 大流行之前和期间幼儿 疫苗接种 的常规吸收:一项基于人群的 队列研究 行政数据研究。

Routine early childhood vaccination uptake before and during the COVID-19 pandemic in Scotland: A population-based cohort study of administrative data.
作者： Chloe Doreen Smith, Iain Hardie, Bonnie Auyeung
摘要中文翻译目标:COVID-19 大流行改变了许多家庭的工作模式,并导致人们更加关注 疫苗。这可能影响了儿童早期对 疫苗接种 的吸收。我们利用苏格兰的关联行政健康数据来调查该流行病是否与幼儿 疫苗接种 的吸收变化有关。研究设计:回顾性观察 队列研究 设计检查苏格兰基于人口的个人层面关联的行政健康数据。方法:我们评估了六种常规 疫苗接种 - 6 合 1 疫苗、肺炎球菌结合物 疫苗 (PCV)、轮状病毒 疫苗、Hib/MenC 疫苗、麻疹、腮腺炎和风疹 (MMR) 的及时摄取情况疫苗 和 PCV 加强剂 - 在苏格兰 12 个月或 24 个月大的儿童中,2019 年 1 月至 2021 年 12 月(N = 199,574 名儿童)。逻辑回归模型评估了大流行病 队列研究 与大流行前 队列研究 的吸收情况,并对关键社会人口统计学变量进行了调整和不调整。交互分析评估了关联是否因地区贫困、父母社会经济阶层、城乡居住地和母亲种族群体而异。结果:在大流行期间,六个 疫苗 中的五个的 疫苗接种 吸收率要么保持稳定,要么有所增加。大多数相互作用在统计上并不显着,这表明流行病对不同社会人口群体的影响大致相似。然而,在大流行期间,先前存在的巨大不平等现象仍然存在,最贫困地区和管理/专业家庭的接受程度较高,而一些少数民族群体的接受程度较低。结论:苏格兰的儿童免疫计划在大流行期间表现出弹性,但先前存在的不平等现象仍然存在。未来的规划应包括保护早期生命 疫苗 和解决结构性障碍。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了 COVID-19 大流行是否改变了苏格兰幼儿 疫苗接种 的常规吸收,其动机是观察到家庭工作模式的变化以及大流行期间 疫苗 关注度的增加。
设计与对象
一项回顾性观察性 队列研究 研究使用苏格兰的关联行政健康数据来检查 2019 年 1 月至 2021 年 12 月期间年龄达到 12 或 24 个月的儿童的基于人口的个人记录(N = 199,574 名儿童)。
核心方法
使用逻辑回归评估六种常规 疫苗接种 的及时采用情况,以比较流行病和大流行前的 队列研究,并调整社会人口统计学变量和跨地区剥夺、父母社会经济阶层、城乡居住地和母亲种族群体的相互作用分析。
主要结果
在大流行期间,六个 疫苗 中的五个的 疫苗接种 吸收率要么保持稳定,要么有所增加,并且大多数交互项在统计上不显着,表明不同社会人口群体的影响大致相似。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为苏格兰儿童免疫计划在大流行期间恢复力的证据,同时指出社会经济和种族群体之间仍然存在巨大的不平等。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,常规幼儿 疫苗接种 计划可以在突发公共卫生事件期间维持覆盖范围,但需要有针对性的战略来解决结构性障碍并减少持续存在的不平等。
局限与证据强度
该研究的观察设计和对行政数据的依赖限制了因果推断,虽然大样本量增强了精度,但无法测量的混杂因素和对其他环境的普遍性仍然令人担忧。

Routine early childhood vaccination uptake before and during the COVID-19 pandemic in Scotland: A population-based cohort study of administrative data.

Authors: Chloe Doreen Smith, Iain Hardie, Bonnie Auyeung
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: The COVID-19 pandemic changed the working patterns of many families and led to an increased focus on vaccines. This may have influenced early childhood vaccination uptake. We utilised linked administrative health data for Scotland to investigate whether the pandemic was associated with changes in uptake of early childhood vaccinations. STUDY DESIGN: Retrospective observational cohort design examining population-based individual-level linked administrative health data for Scotland. METHODS: We assessed timely uptake of six routine vaccinations - 6-in-1 vaccine, pneumococcal conjugate vaccine (PCV), rotavirus vaccine, Hib/MenC vaccine, measles, mumps and rubella (MMR) vaccine and PCV booster - among children reaching 12 months or 24 months of age in Scotland, January 2019-December 2021 (N = 199,574 children). Logistic regression modelling assessed uptake among a pandemic cohort compared to a pre-pandemic cohort, with and without adjustment for key sociodemographic variables. Interaction analysis assessed whether associations varied across area deprivation, parental socioeconomic class, urban-rural residence, and maternal ethnic group. RESULTS: Vaccination uptake either remained stable or increased for five of the six vaccines during the pandemic. Most interactions were not statistically significant, indicating broadly similar pandemic effects across sociodemographic groups. However, large pre-existing inequalities remained during the pandemic, with least-deprived areas and managerial/professional households having higher uptake, and several minority ethnic groups having lower uptake. CONCLUSIONS: Scotland's childhood immunisation programme showed resilience during the pandemic, but pre-existing inequalities persisted. Future planning should include safeguarding early-life vaccines and addressing structural barriers.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed whether the COVID-19 pandemic altered routine early childhood vaccination uptake in Scotland, motivated by observed changes in family working patterns and increased vaccine focus during the pandemic.
Design and population
A retrospective observational cohort study used linked administrative health data for Scotland to examine population-based individual-level records of children reaching 12 or 24 months of age between January 2019 and December 2021 (N = 199,574 children).
Core methods
Timely uptake of six routine vaccinations was assessed using logistic regression to compare pandemic and pre-pandemic cohorts, with adjustment for sociodemographic variables and interaction analysis across area deprivation, parental socioeconomic class, urban-rural residence, and maternal ethnic group.
Main results
Vaccination uptake either remained stable or increased for five of the six vaccines during the pandemic, and most interaction terms were not statistically significant, indicating broadly similar effects across sociodemographic groups.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence of resilience in Scotland’s childhood immunisation programme during the pandemic, while noting that large pre-existing inequalities persisted across socioeconomic and ethnic groups.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that routine early childhood vaccination programmes can maintain coverage during public health emergencies, but targeted strategies are needed to address structural barriers and reduce persistent inequalities.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s observational design and reliance on administrative data limit causal inference, and while the large sample size strengthens precision, unmeasured confounding and generalisability to other settings remain concerns.
